2026-11-01T02:09:40.2205872Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 14 with the V-prepack and sum-only Prefill FA3 candidates behind one decomposed 12-GPU in-job gate

Round 13 (41253426202) rejected native V plus four-chain sum on pair
calls: on all 12 H200s the timed writer plus FA3 plus O was 46-52 us per
call slower than the round-12 pair row. That gate timed only the
composite, so the loss could not be assigned. The gpt-6-astra diagnosis
points at three suspects without being able to size them: the native
writer turned each warp's two 64-bit V stores into sixteen byte stores
(about 8x the sector requests, plus more Q/K integer work); the
Transpose_V=false load path moves the V load behind the K acquire; and
the identity-PRMT lifetime fix joins on every P word (+22 instructions
for sum alone). Serving kept round 12's selection and repeated it at
9947.50.

This round keeps the round-12 pair row as control and offers two
independent candidates for the same pair calls (two requests, each
3900-4096 tokens). The implementation is 85636aa by Codex; this commit
only enables it.

- P, first choice: the old FP32 writer stays, then a new pack op builds
  the native V image once from the contiguous V with six 128-bit stores
  per thread (contiguous 512 B per warp store, 16 sector requests), then
  native-only FA3 (variant 41184, no sum). It moves the V transpose from
  56,784 KV visits to 336 unique blocks: about 1.396 GB of logical shared
  traffic leaves FA3, while the pack adds about 8.2 MB of global traffic,
  16.5 MB of shared traffic and one launch. Paged K/V are written only
  by the old writer.
- S, second choice: old writer and old V with the lifetime-keeping sum
  (variant 24800), FADD depth 23 to 7, +22 steady instructions.
- Diagnostics, on 3994+3994 only and never adopted: writer-only (old,
  native, old plus pack, pack alone), fixed-input FA3-only for control,
  S, native V and the round-13 composite, the composite path for all
  five, the interaction term and O alone. Raw samples are kept so that
  the round-13 loss can be assigned whatever this round adopts.

CPU proof under CUDA 13.4, sm_90a (the image uses CUDA 13.0.88):
existing FA3 controls and both old writer index types are
encoded-SASS identical, no C75xx diagnostics, P's FA3 has no spill, S
matches the proven sum-only SASS, and the pack uses 35 registers, 24,576
B shared and no spill.

The gate group (scripts/select-firmus-prefill-fa3-prepack.py) runs once,
after the round-12 pair gate adopted, on the same 12 GPUs. It runs the
diagnostics, then P's full gate, then S's if time remains. Each full
gate needs:
- byte checks of the V image, Q/K and paged KV first;
- the seven pairs, each saving at least 5 us per call on every GPU in
  the forward, reverse and pooled medians of writer plus FA3 plus O;
- FP32 causal reference with relative L2 at most 0.05 and max scaled
  error at most 0.25, plus two changed-input replays;
- singles 3994, 4096 and 3584 byte-equal to the round-12 path;
- 1200 back-to-back serving-path calls byte-equal to the first.
If both pass, P is adopted; P and S are never combined. The env line
changes only when 12 distinct H200 UUIDs with one run ID and one image
hash all pass. Otherwise round 12's selection serves.

Time: one 180 s wall budget for the whole group, counted from its start
and including cold startup, with srun bounded at start plus 175 s and
the aggregate at start plus 180 s. Against round 13's 741 s job, with
its 62 s vsum wall replaced and the pair gate allowed its full 155 s,
the job comes to about 957 s of the 1020 s limit.

This commit sets prefill.fa3_prepack_gate to true and flips the test
that pinned it false; fa3_pair_gate stays on. The image pin stays at
a8232b16 (41201412459); the fresh-build provision rewrites the runtime
pin from this build.

Validation: the full pytest suite exits 0 on this tree, with no failures; Codex reported 1374 passed and 78 skipped before the flip.
